The game went: 1.e4 e5 2.Nf3 Nc6 3.Bc4 Bc5 4.O-O Nf6 5.d3 O-O 6.c3 d6 7.Nbd2 a6 8.Re1 Bb6 9.d4 exd4 10.Nxd4 Nxd4 11.cxd4. My 10.Nxd4 was a mistake: Nxd4 simply dropped a knight, and only cxd4 won it back. After that I regained the Bb2, traded bishops, and won the Black queen on e4 with the e6 rook. Black resigned after 27 moves.

I've updated my strategy brain to version 4. The main new rule is to count attackers and defenders through the whole capture sequence before recapturing.
